# TASK: rewrite 1318 weak wiki summaries for the game Thornvael (one long job, done in batches)

You are a lore editor for the in-game wiki of **Thornvael**, an original dark-fantasy top-down RTS/ARPG: a frontier border region, ash, iron, salt-tithe armies, gnolls, necromancers, the Vanguard Company, Thornwick levy-troops. Tone: grim, dry, concrete, slightly wry, like a quartermaster's ledger or a field guide. English only.

## What to write
For EVERY entry in the ENTRIES list at the bottom write:
- "summary": one line, 70-140 characters. What the thing IS and why a player cares. Use only facts from that entry's infobox (tier, slot, class, effect, act). Never invent or contradict numbers; do not copy stats verbatim.
- "flavor": 2-3 sentences, 220-420 characters of in-world lore: who made, used or feared it, where it turns up in the Border.

## Rules
- No story spoilers (act endings, deaths, final boss identity). No names from Rage of Mages, Diablo, Warcraft or other existing games. No emojis, no markdown inside strings.
- Keep the title unchanged. At most one or two small new proper nouns per entry.
- Same tier/kind entries must sound different; no two entries start with the same two words.
- Category hints: sets = a matched gear set (describe the unit that wears it); bestiary = a creature (behaviour, where it lurks, what it does to the player); gems = socketable stone; skills = a trainable ability; affixes = a magic prefix/suffix (describe the craft or curse that gives it); uniques = a one-of-a-kind relic with a history; lore = a chronicle/place/faction/figure article (write the summary as a teaser, flavor as the opening lines); items = ordinary gear/consumables.
- Cover every id exactly once, in the order given. Never skip, merge or rename ids.

## HOW TO WORK (important - follow exactly)
1. You cannot output everything in one reply, so work in batches of **250 entries**, in list order. Start now with entries 1-250. Do not ask questions, do not summarise the task, do not wait.
2. Each reply contains ONLY: one JSON object inside a single ```json code block, then one final line REPORT.
3. JSON format: {"<id>": {"summary": "...", "flavor": "..."}, ...}  Straight double quotes, no trailing commas, no line breaks inside strings, quotes escaped.
4. REPORT line, outside the code block, exactly: REPORT: done=<ids written so far>/1318 | last_id=<last id> | next_start=<first id not yet written or NONE>
5. When I reply "continue" (or just "c"), output the next 250 entries the same way, without repeating earlier ids. If I paste nothing else, assume "continue".
6. If you are running out of quota or length, finish the current entry, close the JSON, and add "| stopped=<reason>" to REPORT. Never leave a batch half-written.
7. Silent self-check before each reply: every id of the batch present once, summary 70-140 chars, flavor 220-420 chars, JSON parses, no number in a summary that is not in that entry's infobox.

## Style sample (tone only, do not reuse wording)
summary: "Pitted pike-head the levy hands out when the real steel runs short."
flavor: "Struck from scrap in the Thornwick forge sheds, these blades go to recruits who have not yet earned a name. The quartermaster logs each one twice: once going out, and once, if luck allows, coming back."
